nodes.

simultaneous failure 
(disk/node).

implemented using 
replication or erasure 
coding.

failure shall exist across 
nodes, disks, or 
controllers.

and such 
procurement should 
not be constrained to 
a single-vendor 
model. 
Furthermore, the use 
of the term "Unified 
Platform" suggests a 
preference for a 
single vendor 
delivering an 
integrated OSS and 
BSS solution. This 
approach may limit 
flexibility and 
competition. We 
request you to 
change the current 
clause as "standards 
API based integration 
between OSS, BSS 
and other adjunct 
functions (like GIS, 
TTS etc)." instead of 
"operating as a 
unified platform."

10. An ISP/ TSP 
license holder for 
Gujarat LSA shall be 
ineligible to 
participate.  
1. Removal of the 
Clause 
 
The intent of this 
clause appears to be 
to prevent 
participation of 
entities that directly 
compete with BSNL 
as Telecom Service 
Providers (TSPs). 
However, the 
present tender has 
been floated by an 
SPV and does not 
involve direct 
competition with 
TSPs/ISPs. 
 
Accordingly, the 
clause unnecessarily 
restricts the 
participation of 
major Public Sector 
Undertakings and 
established telecom 
infrastructure 
providers such as 
RailTel and other 
qualified telecom 
entities. This may 
No change. The provision 
shall remain as per the 
RFP.

Section 10 - Annexure 
B  
XI. 
Minimum 
Technical 
specificati
ons for 
Uninterru
pted 
Power 
Supply 
(UPS) 
System at 
GP (page 
no. 437) 
Electrical Safety - 
Isolation transformer 
for Source isolation 
Please suggest 
Isolation transformer 
to be placed in input 
side or at output side 
of UPS. As isolation 
transformer placed 
at either side shall 
provide necessary 
protection and safety 
Preferablly at source, The 
end objective is to 
safeguard networking 
equipment, the safest 
practice is to isolate all 
upstream sources (AC 
input, bypass source, UPS 
output, and battery 
source as applicable) 
rather than relying on the 
UPS operating mode 
alone. bidder is advised 
to follows standard 
electrical safety and 
LOTO(Lockout/Tagout) 
principles and prevents 
back-feed or unexpected 
energization.

considered as 3,000 
devices only.
